Biederman drafts generic revoked file operations on 2009-04-12 and takes BSD semantics as the model for Linux: closed pipes and hung ttys read as zero, matching what BSD returns after sys_revoke, so he states "I am just going to pick my best understanding of what BSD did." Linux sys_revoke mirrors BSD. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1087811 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2008年6月 Rik van Riel 在 -mm 的 noreclaim LRU 基础设施评审中提出直接沿用 BSD 术语为不可回收页命名,引语 Want to reuse the BSD term 'pinned' instead?,Andrew Morton 则要求避免复用已有 reclaimable 语义,反映 Linux VM 新增页标志时显式借用 BSD 术语命名。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1087968 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2009年4月 revoked file operations RFC 讨论中, Eric Biederman 陈述 BSD 系统中文件被 revoke 后读操作的实际返回行为, 引语 that is what bsd returns after a sys_revoke, Jamie Lokier 据此争论 EOF 与 EIO 默认值取舍, 显示 Linux VFS 文件语义设计参照 BSD 行为。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1088354 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2000年12月 Daniel Phillips 在与 Matthew Dillon 关于 FreeBSD 页清理守护进程的讨论中比较两套内存管理, 引语 working on FreeBSD's pageout daemon, 指出 FreeBSD 让脏页在 inactive 队列多走一轮从而让干净页优先被清理, Linux VM 演进同期直接与该实现对话。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1088387 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2012年4月 Yandex 工程师 Alexey Ivanov 报告迁移过程中周期性任务反复触发已映射页回收, 引语 In progress of migration from FreeBSD to Linux and we found some strange behavior, 并追溯 reclaim_mapped 支持在 LRU 拆分为 anon 与 file 后被移除, 提出 FreeBSD 与 Linux 回收策略差异比较。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1088484 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2001年8月 Rik van Riel 的 2.4.8-ac5 VM 补丁把 /proc/sys/vm 的页老化策略做成可切换参数, 引语 2) linear decline: Linux 2.0, FreeBSD, 直接把 FreeBSD 的线性页老化算法列为可选模式, 是 Linux 2.4 VM 与 BSD 页老化设计的显式谱系证据。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1089398 -->`
- 【Linux社区】FreeBSD pageout daemon 算法讨论: Daniel Phillips 与 Matt Dillon 比较 inactive_dirty 队列与脏页清洗优先级, 属 BSD 与 Linux 页回收谱系对比。原文: "it still looks like he's keeping dirty pages 'on probation' twice as long as before" `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1081374 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Vlastimil Babka 讨论 PG_zero 后台清零时引用 BSD 内核先例, 称某 BSD 内核于 2016 年移除该功能并给出 DragonFly BSD 提交链接, 属跨内核 BSD 谱系证据。原文: "one BSD kernel actually removed this functionality in 2016" `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1082082 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Rik van Riel 剖析 FreeBSD vm_glue.c 的 load control 与进程换出, 并对比早期 BSD 的限速时钟算法与 FreeBSD、NetBSD、Linux 的现代做法, BSD 谱系证据强。原文: "FreeBSD's load control code (the process swapping in vm_glue.c) can never work in many scenarios" `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1083181 -->`
- 【Linux社区】madvise(MADV_FREE) 手册文本许可证讨论: 指出 BSD man page 文本可自由引用而 Solaris 文本许可不明, 属 BSD 许可证谱系史料。原文: "the initially proposed text was from the BSD man page (which would be okay)" `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1083476 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Matt Dillon 在 FreeBSD 页回收讨论中回应 Rik van Riel, 指出 FreeBSD 存在 maxscan 等价机制且颠簸检测有效, 属 BSD 与 Linux 页扫描限速谱系史料。原文: "That explains why FreeBSD's thrashing detection code works" `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1083881 -->`
- 【Linux社区】MADV_WIPEONFORK 讨论中用户态加密维护者引用 OpenBSD arc4random.h 与 arc4random.c 作为 DRBG fork 后重初始化的参考实现, 属 BSD 代码谱系证据。原文: "and here's how LibreSSL does it" `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1084265 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Jeff Xu 的 mseal() 提案被 Matthew Wilcox 直接对标 BSD 的 immutable 概念反驳，称 Linux 试图实现等价语义却没理解要封锁的概念。原文："it will be equivalent to BSD's immutable()." Wilcox 结论："You don't know what you're doing." 这是 Linux mm 侧显式引用 BSD 用户态记忆保护 API 谱系的罕见讨论。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1242033 -->`
- 【Linux社区】OpenBSD 首席维护者 Theo de Raadt 在 mseal() v10 讨论中直接反驳 Linus 关于 BSD 原子性规则的推测，并强烈抗议人身攻击。原文："It is not a BSD thing.  I searched many kernels." "CAN YOU PLEASE SHUT IT WITH THE PERSONAL ATTACKS?" 该邮件是 BSD 治理人物在 linux-mm 上就 BSD 谱系与治理冲突正面发声的直接史料。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1247576 -->`
- 【Linux社区】FreeBSD 开发者 Mateusz Guzik 在 Linux percpu 缓存讨论中直接引用 FreeBSD 每 CPU 分配器作为可比参照，评价其在规模扩展上的效果。原文："FreeBSD has a per-cpu allocator which pretends to be the same as the slab allocator" 并补充 "Scales perfectly fine of course since it caches objs per-CPU"，这是 Linux mm/percpu 线程中罕见的 FreeBSD 谱系直接对读。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1248522 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Rafael Aquini 为 memory_hotplug 补丁集背书,其测试环境源自 FreeBSD 机器提交的 PPC 启动崩溃报告:测试链接指向 optiplex-fbsd 域名,并转述 Everything has worked like a charm, AFAICT. 该 FreeBSD 主机的崩溃报告成为上游 Linux 补丁的验证基线,体现 BSD 系发行版在 Linux 内存子系统问题发现中的外部输入作用。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1109377 -->`
- 【Linux社区】OpenBSD 维护者 Theo de Raadt 于 2024-02-01 在 Linux 内核 mseal 引入补丁讨论中直接参与,反驳 Linus Torvalds 认为 mprotect 与 PROT_SEAL 可等价合并的论断,并指出 if an error is returned, we know nothing was changed 这一原子性语义原则,mprotect 加 sealed 页后 errno 集合会因 EPERM 而变化。BSD 谱系人物在此直接参与 Linux 系统调用语义设计,属 BSD 人物参与 Linux 治理的典型案例。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1112566 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Michal Hocko 就 memory_hotplug 补丁集感谢 Rafael Aquini 的评审与测试,引用对方测试来源为 FreeBSD 机器提交的 PPC 启动崩溃报告链接,原文转述 Everything has worked like a charm, AFAICT. 说明上游 Linux 内存热插拔修复的验证基线来自 FreeBSD 生态用户 Nico 报告的问题,构成 BSD 系发行版向 Linux 内核贡献问题发现与回归验证的链条。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1113224 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Theo de Raadt 就 mseal 引入 RFC 与 Linus Torvalds 交换意见,转述 OpenBSD mprotect 手册页 CAVEATS 中 less permissive 的单向降权行为,并披露我方 chrome fork 已使用 mimmutable,Robert Nagy 维护 1280 patches to make chrome work on OpenBSD 而被 Google 忽略上游化。此为 BSD 派生实现经验输入 Linux 页保护语义设计的直接证据。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1114520 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Mateusz Guzik 在 execve 扩展性讨论中援引 FreeBSD 的 per-cpu slab 分配器作为 Linux 侧设计先例, 属 BSD 实现影响 Linux 设计的证据。引语: "FreeBSD has a per-cpu allocator which pretends to be the same as the slab allocator, except handing out per-cpu bufs." 其给出的 4/8/16/32/64 尺寸缓存直接启发 percpu 预分配池思路。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1153928 -->`
- 【Linux社区】David Hildenbrand 在 ANON_LARGE_FOLIOS 命名与语义争论中引用 FreeBSD 官方状态报告, 指出 FreeBSD 将此类页称为 Medium-sized superpages, 构成 BSD 术语进入 Linux THP 语义边界讨论的一例。引语: "Apparently, freebsd wants to call these things Medium-sized superpages" 并附 freebsd.org/status 链接。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1154818 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Jeff Xu 的 mseal() RFC 覆盖信将 Linux 新系统调用定位为 BSD 与 XNU 既有机制的移植, 明确 OpenBSD mimmutable 为对等先例。引语: "A similar feature already exists in the XNU kernel with the VM_FLAGS_PERMANENT flag and on OpenBSD with the mimmutable syscall" 属 BSD 设计影响 Linux API 设计。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1155602 -->`
- 【Linux社区】OpenBSD 创始人 Theo de Raadt 在 linux-mm 直接参与 mseal 评审, 提出 sigaltstack() 与 MAP_STACK 强制结合构成 mseal 绕过面, 并给出 OpenBSD 官方策略。引语: "In OpenBSD, sigaltstack() forces MAP_STACK onto the specified (pre-allocated) region" 属 BSD 人物与 BSD 安全语义双向影响 Linux。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1156215 -->`
- 【Linux社区】mseal() 讨论串中 Jeff Xu 转述覆盖信同一段先例陈述, OpenBSD mimmutable 与 XNU VM_FLAGS_PERMANENT 被列为 Linux sealing 语义的对标来源。引语: "A similar feature already exists in the XNU kernel with the VM_FLAGS_PERMANENT flag and on OpenBSD with the mimmutable syscall" `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1156238 -->`
- 【Linux社区】同一 FreeBSD per-cpu 分配器引用在 percpu 计数器分配构想中再次出现, 显示 FreeBSD slab 模拟层是 Linux percpu 分配讨论反复援引的参照。引语: "pretends to be the same as the slab allocator, except handing out per-cpu bufs. So far it has sizes 4, 8, 16, 32 and 64" 作者将其作为避免碎片化的对标方案。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1159348 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Jeff Xu 在 mseal 文档评审中直接对比 BSD immutable 与 Linux 多级 sealing, 并引用 OpenBSD mprotect(2) man 页说明 immutable 页允许 RW 降为 RO 这一 BSD 语义细节。引语: "I want to know why we don't just do the BSD immutable thing, and why we need this multi-level sealing thing." 是 BSD 谱系影响 Linux API 最直接的论证。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1159839 -->`
- 【Linux社区】mseal() RFC 串再次出现同一 BSD 先例段落, 说明该对标关系贯穿整轮评审。引语: "already exists in the XNU kernel with the VM_FLAGS_PERMANENT flag and on OpenBSD with the mimmutable syscall" 后续争论焦点即 Linux 多比特分级 sealing 与 BSD 单比特 immutable 的取舍。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1160092 -->`
- 【Linux社区】mseal 系统调用评审中出现 OpenBSD 创始人 Theo de Raadt 直接介入，邮箱域名 deraadt@openbsd.org，原话: 'I personally do not like the heap getting sealed like that.' BSD 阵营对 Linux 用户态内存封禁接口的堆语义给出了明确反对意见。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1169142 -->`
- 【Linux社区】LSFMMBPF Lazy RCU 议题提案中明确点名 FreeBSD 实现，作者写道 'FreeBSD does this for their equivalent of RCU, for instance'，并提议把延迟释放集成进 Linux slab，构成 FreeBSD 做法的直接跨系统借鉴。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1171138 -->`
- 【Linux社区】x86 core dump XSAVE note 讨论中 FreeBSD 核心开发者 John Baldwin 现身并提供跨系统实测差异，原话: 'FreeBSD dumps an XSAVE register set note that matches the same layout'。BSD 发行版在真实硬件上的行为被直接引用于 Linux ABI 设计辩论。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1172137 -->`
- 【Linux社区】UDF 文件系统 SPDX 许可证转换时发现 fs/udf/ecma_167.h 头部 'looks like 2-clause BSD Source-Code license' 但其第二条取自 3-Clause BSD 的第三条，构成无法用 SPDX 标识符表达的 BSD 非标准变体，因此被排除在转换范围外。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1172480 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Bagas Sanjaya 2023-05-22 提交的 UDF 文件系统 SPDX 转换系列, 直接讨论 BSD 许可证变体的识别困难: The latter file apparently looks like 2-clause BSD Source-Code license, yet the second clause is from third clause of 3-Clause BSD. 该自定义许可无法用 SPDX 标识符表达, 故 fs/udf/ecma_167.h 未转换. BSD 许可证在 Linux 内核树中的遗留与分类. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1190154 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Linus Torvalds 于 2023-10-17 回复 Theo de Raadt 关于 mseal 系统调用的 RFC 讨论, de Raadt 的 openbsd.org 邮箱使其以 OpenBSD 代表身份介入 Linux 内核设计对话. Linus 采纳其命名建议: I do like us starting with just mimmutable(), since it already exists. 该 syscall 最终以 mseal 合入, 而 mimmutable 谱系直接来自 OpenBSD. 属于 BSD 人物参与 Linux 治理与 BSD 技术特征移植. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1191048 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Jeff Xu (Chrome) 2024-04-15 提交 mseal 补丁 v10, 提案说明中显式对比 BSD 家族已有实现: A similar feature already exists in the XNU kernel with the VM_FLAGS_PERMANENT flag and on OpenBSD with the mimmutable syscall. Chrome 为 CFI 工作采纳该特性. mseal 的 BSD 谱系在此被明确记录. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1191399 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Ryan Roberts 2023-10-10 关于变长 folio 命名争论, 引用同行指出的 BSD 命名惯例: I think FreeBSD tends to call it Medium-sized superpages. 该讨论涉及 Linux THP 术语与 FreeBSD 术语体系的可比性. 属于 BSD 术语传统进入 Linux 命名空间. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1194272 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Jeff Xu 2024-01-31 提交 mseal v8, 提案对比说明明确记录 BSD 家族先行实现: A similar feature already exists in the XNU kernel with the VM_FLAGS_PERMANENT flag and on OpenBSD with the mimmutable syscall. mseal 由此确立其 BSD 谱系来源. 属于 BSD 技术特征移植. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1194430 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Theo de Raadt 2024-01-24 以 deraadt@openbsd.org 邮箱直接参与 mseal 系统调用补丁评审, 就 MAP_SEALABLE 与内存库边界问题提出反对: you start telling all open source library authors to always use MAP_SEALABLE. 其论述直接关联 OpenBSD mimmutable 语义. BSD 人物参与 Linux 内核治理. 属于 BSD 人物与治理候选. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1194627 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2024年5月mseal()合并讨论中，Matthew Wilcox指出该拟议系统调用实为OpenBSD既有机制的移植而非Chrome专属需求。原文：This version of mseal() is OpenBSD's mimmutable() with a basically unused extra flags argument，并以此作为其在Chrome之外同样有用之存在证明。属BSD谱系派生。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1261401 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2024年5月mseal()合并线程中，Linus Torvalds直接回应OpenBSD维护者Theo de Raadt提出的非原子性担忧，拒绝为其修改内核。原文：So no, we're not making gratuitous changes for stupid reasons. 该回击对象为BSD阵营核心维护者，构成跨内核治理互动。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1261600 -->`
- 【Linux社区】mseal()补丁第8版评审中，作者Jeff Xu转引OpenBSD维护者Theo de Raadt的反对意见。原文：I personally do not like the heap getting sealed like that. 并由此展开对mprotect()在遇到已密封区段时返回失败而不作半程修改的原子性论证。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1261780 -->`
- 【Linux社区】变阶匿名大页补丁第6版评审中，David Hildenbrand在为页面尺寸命名争论时援引FreeBSD术语。原文：I think FreeBSD tends to call it Medium-sized superpages. 以FreeBSD命名习惯作为Linux内存管理术语选择的对照依据。属BSD谱系。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1263292 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2024年5月mseal()讨论中，Liam R. Howlett转引Theo de Raadt就mprotect()语义逐段引用POSIX规范。原文：An implementation may permit accesses other than those specified by prot。该争论以BSD维护者与Linus Torvalds隔空交锋为轴线。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1263387 -->`
- 【Linux社区】mseal()补丁第7版封面说明中，作者Jeff Xu明确将设计依据溯源至其他内核。原文：A similar feature already exists in the XNU kernel with the VM_FLAGS_PERMANENT flag and on OpenBSD with the mimmutable syscall. Linux实现系派生于OpenBSD。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1263979 -->`
- 【Linux社区】David Hildenbrand critiques the mm/gmem vm_object patch, warning that it introduces 'controversial BSD design concepts into Linux' from FreeBSD VM, arguing it adds complexity and feels like a regression. The patch adds struct vm_object to track address mapping status using xarray, originated from FreeBSD VM. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1265932 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Theo de Raadt, founder of OpenBSD, participates in the Linux mseal syscall discussion. Eric Biggers critiques the patch as 'misleading, as it claims to just be adding a new syscall' while it actually adds three new UAPIs. The thread debates PROT_SEAL versus MAP_SEALABLE flag design. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1266513 -->`
- 【Linux社区】In the virtio balloon stats RFC, zhenwei pi notes 'FreeBSD supports virtio balloon for a long time' and that OOM kill is used on FreeBSD too, raising the question of how Linux-specific these new virtio events should be described and whether the schema is portable enough. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1266927 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Jeff Xu's mseal() RFC for Linux explicitly references 'on OpenBSD with the mimmutable syscall' and the XNU kernel's VM_FLAGS_PERMANENT as prior art for memory sealing, which protects mappings from modification to mitigate memory corruption bugs and enforce CFI guarantees. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1268484 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Pedro Falcato, a FreeBSD developer, argues against removing splice from the page cache, citing 'https://people.freebsd.org/~gallatin/talks/euro2019-ktls.pdf' as evidence that zerocopy sendfile remains important even with TLS offloading via NIC hardware, warning of breaking high-bandwidth encrypted network paths. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1274031 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Mateusz Guzik, a FreeBSD kernel developer, proposes reverting Linux's PID IDR API to a sparse bitmap plus hash table, noting 'what FreeBSD is doing and it gets better scalability' for clone operations, contrasting Linux's IDR choice with FreeBSD's superior data structure for process ID management. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1275202 -->`
- 【Linux社区】David P. Reed, an OS designer who started 'with Multics in 1970' the direct ancestor of Unix and BSD, critiques Linux userfaultfd as architecturally incoherent, calls the Linux kernel contributors 'poorly organized and chaotic', and questions who the decision makers are for userfaultfd functionality. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1277064 -->`
- 【Linux社区】In the khugepaged mTHP discussion, Kiryl Shutsemau references 'the FreeBSD reservation system comes in handy' for best-effort THP allocation without committing to mapping, noting that memory reclaim could break THPs down when needed, which David Hildenbrand dismisses as only downsides without benefit. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1277151 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Linux 新增 mseal() 内存封禁系统调用 RFC 中，提案者明确把现有 BSD 谱系实现列为同类参照: 'in the XNU kernel with the
> VM_FLAGS_PERMANENT [3] flag and on OpenBSD with the mimmutable syscall [4].' Matthew Wilcox 逐条评审批注。XNU 与 OpenBSD 两套 BSD 派生实现被直接引为 Linux mm 接口的跨系统先例。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1178814 -->`
- 【Linux社区】OpenBSD 创始人 Theo de Raadt 就 Linux mmap PROT_SEAL 记忆密封语义直接介入并否决，Jeff Xu 在 mseal() v7 补丁集中回应: 'I have never looked at one line of openBSD code, prototype
or not, nor did I install openBSD before.' BSD 阵营对 Linux 用户态内存密封接口给出了明确的跨系统反对意见，并附以一年工程化实测依据。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1179450 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Theo de Raadt 审阅 Linux mseal.rst 文档时以 OpenBSD 既有行为作为判定基准: 'In OpenBSD, mimmutable blocks all those madvise() operations.' 并质疑补丁作者对匿名内存破坏性 madvise 行为的表述立场不明。BSD 实现的实际行为被直接引为 Linux mm 文档的评审标尺。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1180346 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Andrew Morton 收编 Nathan Chancellor 的 hexagon timer-regs.h 清理补丁，被删除的头文件首行带 SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only 与 Linux Foundation 版权行，展示了 Linux 内存树内 SPDX GPL 许可标识的标准放置形态，是 Linux 与 BSD 许可证对照下的许可元数据样本。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1183100 -->`
- 【Linux社区】关于 MAP_32BIT 起始地址 0x40000000 是否武断的讨论中，提问者把 FreeBSD 列为对照系统，写道 another operating system that provides MAP_32BIT, FreeBSD, 指出 FreeBSD 的 mmap 会直接返回 0x10000 且不导致可执行页问题，据此反推 Linux 该下限缺少技术必要性。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1184231 -->`
- 【Linux社区】匿名内存可变阶 folio 命名之争中，David Hildenbrand 引 FreeBSD 术语 FreeBSD tends to call it Medium-sized superpages 作为先例，指出 small 一词暗示必然小于既有 THP，Ryan Roberts 随即采纳 small-sized THP 命名，显示 FreeBSD 术语在 Linux mm 术语体系中的渗透路径。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1184431 -->`

- 【Linux社区】第三方提交 GCC 扩展 reftrack 用于内核引用计数,Kees Cook 就性能与 UAF 覆盖率追问,并给出上游政策,原话 "For compiler extensions we want things to be upstream in GCC or Clang",称 GCC 插件在内核中已近弃用但可作概念验证;随信所附原型代码头部同时携带 GPL-2.0-only 与 Apache-2.0 两种标识符及个人版权行。属内核编译工具链准入政策与许可证边界的一手记录。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1211216 -->`

- 【Linux社区】mseal 引入讨论。Theo de Raadt 主张 Unix 系统调用必须原子,原话 "Unix system calls must be atomic.";Linus Torvalds 直接反驳 "That's actually not true, and never has been.",以 mlock 先锁定成功再报页面填充失败为例说明原子性只是应追求而非绝对要求,并指出 POSIX 对 write 的原子性描述本身有错,但认可 mseal 个案应保证原子。Jeff Xu 借此确认实现方向。属 BSD 核心维护者与 Linux 最高维护者就 Unix 语义规范直接交锋的治理一手档案,兼 BSD 谱系与人物证据。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1212665 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Mateusz Guzik argues the Linux lockless path is "just scary enough" that he never implemented it, and describes prior FreeBSD prototype work: "I implemented something like this as a demo in FreeBSD few years back, it did not blow up". Lockless RCU dentry-vnode lookup is a known BSD-derived technique in this domain. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1093713 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Theo de Raadt argues Linux mseal must match OpenBSD mimmutable semantics to stay portable, proposing "#define mimmutable(addr, len)  mseal(addr, len, 0)" and noting "on OpenBSD and probably other related BSD operating systems, MADV_DONTNEED is non-destructive". `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1095303 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Jeff Xu defends PROT_SEAL and MAP_SEALABLE while Theo de Raadt pushes for a single portable sealing API, and Xu concedes that the OpenBSD reference is what drew him in. The thread is the only place in the batch where an OpenBSD-derived seal design is negotiated with Linux maintainers. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1096656 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Pedro Falcato cites OpenBSD userland as evidence the sealing API is battle-tested: "if we trivially look at e.g OpenBSD ld.so code, mmap() + mimmutable() and mprotect() + mimmutable() seem like common patterns". `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1099388 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Theo de Raadt from OpenBSD participates directly in the linux-mm mseal syscall thread, confronting Linus Torvalds on whether a partial mprotect() call counts as succeeded when a sealed region is present. Quote: 'So tell me -- did the mprotect() system call succeed or did not it succeed?' This is direct BSD person-level involvement in Linux kernel API governance. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1116401 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Jeff Xu's mseal() syscall RFC explicitly cites OpenBSD mimmutable and XNU VM_FLAGS_PERMANENT as prior art, positioning the new Linux syscall as a follow-on to BSD memory-sealing lineage. Quote: 'A similar feature already exists in the XNU kernel with the VM_FLAGS_PERMANENT flag and on OpenBSD with the mimmutable syscall'. Direct BSD technology inheritance. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1116413 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Theo de Raadt of OpenBSD replies in the mseal documentation RFC thread, defending the original design intent of mimmutable against Jeff Xu's claim that BSD's immutable was tuned only for libc. Quote: 'That is not true. During the mimmutable design I took the entire software ecosystem into consideration. Not just libc.' BSD person asserting design lineage. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1118440 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Pedro Falcato discusses the mseal() syscall RFC, pointing at OpenBSD ld.so's mmap+mimmutable and mprotect+mimmutable patterns as evidence for a single-shot mmap+seal path. Quote: 'if we trivially look at e.g OpenBSD ld.so code, mmap() + mimmutable() and mprotect() + mimmutable() seem like common patterns.' BSD codebase cited as design reference. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1119834 -->`
- 【Linux社区】In the mseal() syscall RFC cover-letter discussion, Pedro Falcato and Jeff Xu review the RFC body that cites OpenBSD as a direct prior-art reference. Quote: 'A similar feature already exists in the XNU kernel with the VM_FLAGS_PERMANENT flag and on OpenBSD with the mimmutable syscall.' BSD kernel API documented as inspiration. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1120540 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Linus 评述 mseal 设计时对比 OpenBSD 的 immutable 语义,认为其允许降低权限是 bug,主张密封即不可再变更。原话: And yes, the OpenBSD semantics of "immutable" apparently allowed reducing permissions, but even the openbsd man-page seems to think that was a bug `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1235018 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Ryan Roberts 在讨论 THP 命名时给出跨系统对照表,把 Linux huge pages、FreeBSD superpages、Windows large pages 并列为同一概念。原话: Larger pages (which we call "huge pages", FreeBSD "superpages", Windows "large pages") `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1237103 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Arnd 分析 personality() 位掩码时列出 Linux 执行域标识,指出设置任一域位会清掉上下位。原话: if any of them are set (BSD/HPUX/IRIX32/IRIX64/...), both the upper and the lower bits are cleared `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1237725 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Theo de Raadt 介入 mseal 讨论坚持系统调用必须原子,回应称 Unix 调用失败须承诺未做任何修改。原话: Unix system calls must be atomic. They either return an error, and that is a promise they made no changes `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1238269 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Linus 回应 Theo de Raadt 的 glibc 兼容性质疑,并回忆 BSD 社区争论风格,Jeff Xu 随后从易用性角度支持 MAP_SEALABLE。原话: I haven't seen the BSD people in so long that I'd forgotten all about it `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1239704 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Linus 反驳 Theo de Raadt 关于 mprotect 部分失败的 POSIX 论证,用实际可复现代码证明行为并非 mseal 特有。原话: Theo, you're making shit up `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1239785 -->`
- 【Linux社区】XSAVE corefile 补丁抄送列表包含 FreeBSD 的 John Herrmann,因 core dump 布局需跨内核与调试器对齐。原话: jhb@FreeBSD.org `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1240144 -->`
- 【Linux社区】John Hubbard 建议将核心 mm 例程改为 GPL 独占导出,理由是模块层无法在仅普通导出的前提下存活。原话: EXPORT_SYMBOL_GPL() seems appropriate and low risk `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1241380 -->`
- 【Linux社区】jfm2@club-internet.fr wrote on 1998-11-25 in the vm-swapin-readahead thread: a daemon can be killed by the system if it runs out of VM, and the reply adds the direct cross-kernel comparison "Same on every BSD." That is the only explicit BSD-family mention in this batch, tying 2.0 OOM behavior to BSD kernels. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1004452 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Theo de Raadt rebuts Jeff Xu for never reading OpenBSD before proposing mseal: "It is obvious to everyone that mseal is a derivative of the mimmutable mechanism". He argues divergence is itself a cost: "Divergence has always been painful." `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1096774 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Linus Torvalds, relaying OpenBSD founder Theo de Raadt review of the Linux mseal() syscall, tells Jeff Xu to change the failure return value from EACESS to EPERM so the new interface aligns with the BSD ancestor mimmutable(), and Xu confirms the change for the next RFC version. Quote: 'that is more-or-less compatible between Linux and OpenBSD?' `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1101248 -->`
- 【Linux社区】In the mseal() design dispute Linus Torvalds answers OpenBSD founder Theo de Raadt and admits he had forgotten the BSD community argumentative style, then agrees with the Raadt objection to the MAP_SEALABLE flag and pivots the design toward PROT_SEAL semantics. Quote: 'I haven't seen the BSD people in so long' `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1101449 -->`
- 【Linux社区】David Hildenbrand writes an OS-independent memory terminology history for THP and folio naming, listing FreeBSD superpages and Windows large pages as the pre-existing terms for larger-than-PMD pages, a BSD-derived vocabulary trace inside Linux mm documentation. Quote: 'FreeBSD superpages, Windows large pages' `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1104412 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Theo de Raadt pushes back on the Linux mseal() documentation, insisting the text must state whether mseal() blocks the same madvise-driven memory trashing pattern that OpenBSD mimmutable() was built to stop, and Jeff Xu replies that mseal does block such patterns. Quote: 'In OpenBSD, mimmutable() solves this problem' `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1104603 -->`
- 【Linux社区】OpenBSD founder Theo de Raadt replies to the Linux mseal() thread against PROT_SEAL, shows the real in-tree OpenBSD use of mimmutable() on the shared-library hints file, and argues that Linux mmap idioms should keep an inspection step before sealing. Quote: 'I'm grepping an entire Unix kernel and userland source tree' `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1104932 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Microsoft engineer Tim Bird replies in the Linux mseal() thread where OpenBSD founder Theo de Raadt had written that Linux developers were not capable of listening, and asks him to keep making technical points without personal attacks, an OpenBSD versus Linux governance moment. Quote: 'It is possible to make your technical points' `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1106574 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Jeff Xu's mseal() syscall RFC v1 cover letter explicitly cites OpenBSD mimmutable and XNU VM_FLAGS_PERMANENT as prior art for the new Linux syscall. Quote: 'A similar feature already exists in the XNU kernel with the VM_FLAGS_PERMANENT flag and on OpenBSD with the mimmutable syscall.' Direct BSD syscall lineage. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1121319 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Theo de Raadt of OpenBSD replies in the mseal() syscall RFC v1 thread, warning that partial sealing is unsafe and contrasting BSD mimmutable experience with the proposed multi-bit API. Quote: 'Partial sealing is not safe. I define partial sealing as blocking munmap, but not mprotect.' BSD person asserting design lineage in Linux kernel API review. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1121975 -->`
- 【Linux社区】OpenBSD 首席架构师 Theo de Raadt 在 mseal 文档评审中以自家系统方案对照 Linux 新系统调用，明确写道：In OpenBSD, mimmutable() solves this problem (in later code iterations)，随后追问 In Linux, does mseal() solve the problem or not，并称若不解决则 it is weaker than mimmutable，构成明确的 BSD 系统到 Linux 的机制谱系对照。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1198678 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Theo de Raadt 直接说明 Linux 新系统调用与他 OpenBSD 成果的关系：mseal() is a clone of mimmutable(2)，并自述 I designed mimmutable(2) in OpenBSD，称最终 API 只有一行地址加长度的形式，同时批评 Linux 版 API 过于复杂，是典型的 BSD 到 Linux 机制移植讨论。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1202778 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Theo de Raadt 在 mseal 系统调用 v8 评审中回述自己多年 OpenBSD 经验的教训，明确写出结论：you can only seal objects which you gaurantee never get recycled，并断言 Pushing a sealed object back into reuse is a disasterous bug，同时批评 Jeff Xu 缺少覆盖各分配器的测试程序来观察真实行为。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1203760 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Theo de Raadt 就 mseal 系列给出 OpenBSD 侧经验总结，指出 Jeff Xu 缺少覆盖各种分配器的测试程序，明确表述 The failure modes described in your docuemnts are not correct，并以你只能密封保证永不回收的对象作为核心告诫，把密封对象重新放回复用池称为灾难性缺陷，同时要求除非理解该约束否则不要调用此接口。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1204841 -->`
- 【Linux社区】David Hildenbrand reviews generic_fadvise() offset validation and cites FreeBSD as the precedent: "This is also the path FreeBSD chose", linking sys/kern/vfs_syscalls.c. Linux then adopts the same negative-offset rejection plus an overflow guard of the form offset > OFF_MAX - len, a direct FreeBSD-to-Linux semantics migration for the fadvise(2) contract. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1333703 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Christoph Lameter argues Apple isolates allocation for hardening: "basically one slab cache for each type of kmalloc for security reasons", linking Apple's XNU memory-safety blog. Vegard Nossum, an OpenBSD developer, replies with a per-callsite static-cache design, so the thread is a Linux-to-Apple-BSD memory-safety exchange on slab taxonomy. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1334266 -->`
- 【Linux社区】In the 64k base page size on x86 debate, Nadia Chambers recaps per-architecture page-grain pitfalls across s390, ppc64, PA-RISC, SPARC and SHMLBA, closing with "a near-VAX minimum MMU/TLB mapping granularity". The VAX is the platform the original 4.xBSD lineage ran on, so this is a weak but real BSD-heritage architecture signal. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1335252 -->`
- 【Linux社区】On anon_vma root lock contention, David Hildenbrand recalls a rejected upstream proposal: "bring some weird FreeBSD semantics into Linux MM and proposed a secondary tracking structure for anon", then rejects double tracking. A rare direct record of a FreeBSD anon-vma design reaching Linux MM and being refused. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1336793 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Mateusz Guzik frames the Linux iput deadlock against his own background: "Where I'm from (the BSD land) vnodes (the inode equivalent) are the base object if you will". He contrasts BSD umount behaviour, where busy inodes make unmount fail or wait, with Linux dentry handling, which treats them differently. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1339187 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Pedro Falcato answers a sendfile zerocopy objection by citing a FreeBSD talk: "You can do zerocopy from the page cache, even with TLS on top, by having your (fancy) NIC do TLS offloading for you", linking people.freebsd.org/~gallatin/talks/euro2019-ktls.pdf for Linux NIC-TLS design. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1339964 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Debating pidfd spawn templates, Li Chen compares exec-transition flags across BSDs: "FreeBSD has P_INEXEC, OpenBSD has PS_INEXEC, and XNU seems even closer with P_LINTRANSIT". He concludes Linux has no single equivalent, since current in_execve acts only as an LSM hint. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1340472 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Defending a non-migration use of userfaultfd, David P. Reed invokes early OS history: "I grew up doing OS research in the early 1970's", citing Mach at CMU and Multics paging as the lineage that resisted monolithic kernels. Mach at CMU is the kernel from which the BSD Mach releases descended, so this is a BSD-lineage origin account. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1341448 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Matthew Wilcox invokes FreeBSD as precedent in the Linux small-sized THP naming debate, arguing that a size-relative term has already stuck in the BSD world rather than inventing a new acronym. Quote: 'Maybe that's the reason why FreeBSD calls them "medium-sized superpages"'. BSD vocabulary enters Linux kernel API naming. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1131195 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Jeff Xu mseal() syscall RFC v4 cover letter positions the new Linux API as inheriting BSD memory sealing design, naming Apple XNU and OpenBSD as existing prior art for sealing virtual memory ranges at runtime. Quote: 'in the XNU kernel with the VM_FLAGS_PERMANENT [3] flag and on OpenBSD with the mimmutable syscall [4]'. BSD lineage explicitly claimed. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1131232 -->`
- 【Linux社区】John Baldwin, a FreeBSD committer, posts to linux-mm about an x86 ELF core-dump note extension and reports that he discovered the interoperability gap on his own FreeBSD desktop, where GDB would not recognize the FreeBSD XSAVE note. Quote: 'FreeBSD dumps an XSAVE register set note that matches the same layout of NT_X86_XSTATE used by Linux'. BSD kernel behavior as cross-kernel evidence. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1131854 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Theo de Raadt, founder and lead developer of OpenBSD, posts directly in the Linux mseal() documentation RFC thread after Jeff Xu accepts changing the failure code so the syscall is more-or-less compatible between Linux and OpenBSD. Quote: 'On the OpenBSD side, I am close to landing our madvise / msync changes'. BSD chief hands-on in Linux API governance. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1132976 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Linus Torvalds flags a proposed dentry refcount optimization as too scary to start, and FreeBSD developer Mateusz Guzik answers that he built a working demo of the same idea inside FreeBSD, then adds that the FreeBSD version operated directly on vnodes. Quote: 'I implemented something like this as a demo in FreeBSD few years back'. BSD implementation used as feasibility evidence for Linux. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1133514 -->`
- 【Linux社区】David Hildenbrand rejects Weixi Zhu GMEM vm_object proposal partly because it would import object-based paging, and Zhu concedes the concept came straight from FreeBSD virtual memory design. Explicit BSD lineage. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1134402 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Linus Torvalds replies to Jeff Xu mseal documentation RFC and rejects the multi-flag sealing bitmask on the grounds that it duplicates BSD simpler immutable design, demanding a real use case for partial sealing. Quote: 'why we don't just do the BSD immutable thing, and why we need this multi-level sealing thing'. BSD immutable used as the reference design in Linux API governance. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1135387 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Jeff Xu 在 mseal() RFC v1 评审串中援引 OpenBSD ld.so 的 mmap 与 mimmutable 惯用组合作为 Linux 一次性密封映射的先例证据。引语: "OpenBSD ld.so code, mmap() + mimmutable() and mprotect() + mimmutable() seem like common patterns" 是 BSD 语义影响 Linux 密封 API 讨论的一例。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1163962 -->`
- 【Linux社区】mseal() RFC v1 评审串中 Jeff Xu 关于一次性密封映射性能收益的论述再次援引 OpenBSD ld.so 的 mmap 与 mimmutable 组合为惯用先例。引语: "OpenBSD ld.so code, mmap() + mimmutable() and mprotect() + mimmutable() seem like common patterns" 表明 BSD 语义反复进入 Linux 密封 API 设计讨论。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1166858 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Arnd Bergmann 在 alpha 架构 personality 位传播修复评审中指出 Linux personality() 函数返回的 8 位执行域包含 BSD HPUX IRIX32 IRIX64 等 BSD 系语义, 涉及 BSD 兼容层在 Linux 人格标志中的遗留处理。引语: "personality() only returns the lower 8 bits, so if any of them are set (BSD/HPUX/IRIX32/IRIX64/...), both the upper and the lower bits are cleared" `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1167752 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Nadia Chambers 在 64k/16k 基础页大小的 LSF/MM/BPF 讨论中追溯页聚集术语到 BSD 早期虚存工作: "it might have actually been McKusick or Karels, hence the terminology 'page clustering'", 并说明 pgcl 缩写取自 NBPG 的 pg 与 CLSIZE 的 cl, ABI 兼容算法源自 Hugh Dickins 2001 年 larpage 补丁. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1321028 -->`
- 【Linux社区】David Laight 在 ELF 解释器路径支持 $ORIGIN 的补丁讨论中以 NetBSD 的先例论证安全性: "I'm sure that NetBSD refused to implement $ORIGIN because it was full of loopholes.", 并进一步要求内核持有目录 inode 引用、为 openat() 增加相对该目录的 magic 参数. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1321663 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Alexey Dobriyan 纠正 selftests/exec 文件中缺失 SPDX-License-Identifier 的补丁, 认定该文件实际沿用 OpenBSD 许可证模板: "The license is OpenBSD, not ISC.", 并给出 cvsweb.openbsd.org 仓库 checkout/src/share/misc/license.template,v 作为出处依据. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1324195 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Nadia Chambers 在 64k 基础页大小的 LSF/MM/BPF 讨论中延续页聚集脉络, 描述 2003 年移植 larpage 时需要保持 THREAD_SIZE 恒定, 否则核心内核出现除零等错误: "just getting it booting in 2003 needed adjusting things so the THREAD_SIZE stayed constant", 并讨论机械辅助前向移植的处理方式. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1324363 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Mateusz Guzik 在 RWH_RMAP_EXCLUDE 争用补丁讨论中以 FreeBSD 大规模包构建经验论证问题真实性: "I'll say upfront I have extensive experience with this crap on FreeBSD", 并指出 "while FreeBSD is in doubt a less scalable kernel, Linux demonstrated to be suffering from the same problems", 具体到 configure 脚本与 makefile 引发的连续 exec 争用. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1326875 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Kiryl Shutsemau 在 khugepaged mTHP 支持补丁 v11 的讨论中引述 FreeBSD 保留式机制, 承认引文属被反驳的前言: "it might be in these cases especially where the FreeBSD reservation system comes in handy", 指其为尽力分配 THP 但真正映射前不承诺, 回收时可直接拆开, 随后他反驳称短期进程只会付出代价而无收益. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1328668 -->`
- 【Linux社区】OpenBSD 创始人 Theo de Raadt 直接介入 Linux mseal() RFC,从 OpenBSD 经验评估 pkey 封印方案,并指出 x86 的 PKRU 系用户态可写寄存器,构成 BSD 与 Linux 内存不可变机制的对照史料。原文: "SADLY on x86, this is managed with a PKRU userland register which is changeble without any supervisor control" `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1228323 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Linus 以维护者身份否决 fs/kernel_read_file 的重复检测变更,Christian Brauner 随即就未抄送 fsdevel 提出跨子系统评审流程异议,构成内核评审边界与抄送规范的直接治理记录。原文: "Stop adding horrific code for some made-up load that isn't real." `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1228956 -->`
- 【Linux社区】mseal() v9 系列封面信明确把 Linux 新系统调用与 BSD 谱系并列,指出 XNU 已有 VM_FLAGS_PERMANENT 而 OpenBSD 早已落地同名能力,是 BSD 安全特性向 Linux 移植的直接证据。原文: "in the XNU kernel with the VM_FLAGS_PERMANENT [3] flag and on OpenBSD with the mimmutable syscall" `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1229120 -->`
- 【Linux社区】David Howells 在 iov_iter 提取助手系列中披露导出许可的连锁后果,新函数改为仅 GPL 导出会使原非 GPL 接口能力流失,直接触及 BSD 等宽松许可第三方模块的可用性。原文: "functionality will probably become unavailable to non-GPL 3rd party modules in future" `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1229232 -->`
- 【Linux社区】mseal 文档修订线程中 Theo de Raadt 指出 OpenBSD 的 mimmutable 会阻断全部破坏性 madvise 行为,Jeff Xu 据此确认 Linux 侧已覆盖该问题,是两系统封印语义差异的对照证据。原文: "In OpenBSD, mimmutable blocks all those madvise() operations." `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1230528 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Linus 以子系统维护者身份对 fsnotify 预内容权限钩子设下准入规则,把开销举证责任前移给提案方,Amir Goldstein 回应已完成的开销消除工作,是内核准入治理的直接文本。原文: "I'm going to NAK any new fsnotify and permission hooks unless people show that they don't add any overhead." `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1230788 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Liam Howlett 评审 VMA 对齐函数改名时追问导出许可归属,反映维护者对 GPL 导出与版权持有人权限边界的实际认知缺口,与宽松许可代码贡献路径直接相关。原文: "is it okay to export something as gpl or does the copyright holder need to do that" `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1230983 -->`
- 【Linux社区】围绕 nofail 分配语义,Linus 公开宣示 MM 层对越界调用的支配地位并拒绝为驱动兜底,确立了内核分层责任边界,是内核治理权的直接表述。原文: "The MM layer is not some slave to users." `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1231320 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Jakub Kicinski 争取合入 W=1 警告修复被 Linus 以解释不足为由拒绝,并给出面向所有维护者的准入判据,是内核补丁质量门槛治理的直接文本。原文: "So no, I'm certainly not applying unexplained random patches that don't bother to explain the what or the why." `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1232725 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Matthew Wilcox 指出内核缺少运行 smatch 的构建机器人,导致空指针比较类缺陷只能事后修补而无法在维护者接手前拦截,是对内核 CI 覆盖面与缺陷拦截流程的治理性诊断。原文: "I don't know that we have any buildbots that run smatch, and most developers don't" `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1233661 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Jeff Xu 预测 Linux mseal 作用域将超过 BSD 的 mimmutable,Theo de Raadt 以四十年 VM 实践予以反驳,并主张由系统默认阻断而非交由程序自行设置,是 BSD 安全设计哲学的公开表述。原文: "mseal() will have a bigger scope than BSD's mimmutable()." `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1233808 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Linus 就模块加载去重判定修复责任在用户态 udev 而非内核,并提出长期弃用 init_module 的取向,涉及内核对外 ABI 生命周期治理与跨层责任划分。原文: "Long-term, it would be good to just deprecate the old init_module() entirely." `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1233975 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Theo de Raadt 就 mseal 部分生效问题给出 Unix 系统调用必须原子的原则,并以 OpenBSD mimmutable 的既有研究作为判据来源,把 BSD 工程经验带入 Linux 接口评审。原文: "In OpenBSD, all mimmutable() aspects were carefully studied to gaurantee this behaviour." `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1234021 -->`
- 【Linux社区】OpenBSD 创建者 Theo de Raadt 在 mseal 提案中以 BSD 人物身份直接介入 Linux 内存封禁设计,与 Jeff Xu 逐条争辩地址空间控制语义。原文: "It's the same with MAP_MSEALABLE. I don't get it." 属 BSD 人物进入 linux-mm 核心安全特性设计的一线史料。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1252524 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Al Viro 在 vfs.git 分支整合计划中明确点名 BSD 谱系文件系统 ufs,同批还有 sysv 与 minix,即 VFS 层 BSD 文件系统进入 Linux 内核对齐讨论。原文: "Mind if I grab minix/sysv/ufs into a branch in vfs.git?" Christoph Hellwig 进一步指出 minix 需要与 ext2 相同的 set_link 修复。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1253140 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Linus Torvalds 在 mseal 文档 RFC 中与 Theo de Raadt 逐条比对 OpenBSD mimmutable() 语义,并采纳 BSD 返回值惯例以达成跨系统兼容,是 BSD 内存封禁 API 影响 Linux 内核接口设计的一手证据。原文: "we'll have something that is more-or-less compatible between Linux and OpenBSD"。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1255685 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Linux mmap 标志 MAP_STACK 的讨论直接引证 BSD 谱系差异作为设计依据,manpage 与补丁说明均以 BSD 行为定义 Linux 该标志的可移植性目标。原文: "MAP_STACK exists (and has an effect) on some other systems (e.g., some of the BSDs)"。属 BSD 系统行为进入 Linux mmap 语义规范的正面记录。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1255686 -->`
- 【Linux社区】mseal() 系统调用补丁在致谢中明确承认 OpenBSD 的 mimmutable() 实现经验与洞察构成设计输入,Liam Howlett 复述该 BSD 影响,是 BSD 技术经验正式进入 Linux mm 特性致谢记录的强证据。原文: "sharing the experiences and insight gained from implementing mimmutable() in OpenBSD"。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1257299 -->`
- 【Linux社区】John Ericson 在 pidfd 进程构造线程里说 "In my FreeBSD patch, for example, I found there was already a status for a process in exec"，并据此建议 Linux 复用同类状态；他同时自述先在 FreeBSD 实现该功能再转投 Linux，属明确 BSD 谱系对照与 API 借鉴。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1277714 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Linux 开发者讨论每进程页大小时写道 "anonymous memory pointers are stored in some intermediary structure (SunOS and UVM call it amap)"，把 Linux 里匿名内存指针的已知难题追溯到 SunOS 与 UVM 这类 BSD 系经典 Unix 实现。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1278744 -->`
- 【Linux社区】David Hildenbrand 在 anon_vma 锁争用讨论中提到 "We had a proposal least year I think where someone wanted to bring some weird FreeBSD semantics into Linux MM"，即 FreeBSD 的匿名内存语义曾被提议移植进 Linux。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1278989 -->`
- 【Linux社区】zswap 批量压缩补丁系列附带一段 Intel 编写的 enable_iaa.sh 配置脚本，其文件头声明 "#SPDX-License-Identifier: BSD-3-Clause" 与 "#Copyright (c) 2025, Intel Corporation"，是 BSD-3 许可证作为内核上游附带的实际用例。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1279594 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Kees Cook 与 Marco Elver 在 slab 类型隔离讨论中引用 Apple 的 XNU 内存安全博文，指称 "iOS did go the path of creating basically one slab cache for each type of kmalloc for security reasons"，把 XNU 做法当作 Linux 参考先例。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1280642 -->`

- 【Linux社区】Marco Elver 在 slab 类型隔离讨论中引用 Apple 官方 XNU 内存安全文章链接，说 "iOS did go the path of creating basically one slab cache for each type of kmalloc for security reasons"，并据此论证 Linux 可达到的隔离程度。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1285467 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Al Viro 在 inode 引用与 umount 讨论中直言 "Linux VFS is seriously different from Heidemann's-derived ones you'll find in BSD land these days"，又指 Different taxonomy of objects，直接以 BSD 系 vnode 分类学对照 Linux VFS。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1286968 -->`

- 【Linux社区】Linus Torvalds 回应 fallocate 原子性争论时说 POSIX 文档之所以像睡前故事，是因为 "not talking about the Linux big kernel lock, but about old BSD model of single inode lock for all IO"，即旧 BSD 的单 inode 锁模型。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1288888 -->`

- 【Linux社区】John Ericson 回复 Li Chen 说 "Glad to hear my suggestion (and the patch too I linked in the other email, I hope?) was useful"，确认其 FreeBSD 补丁的进程 in exec 状态可复用；另提出 null namespaces 设想，为更隔离的无特权进程提供廉价创建路径。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1291182 -->`
- 【Linux社区】John Ericson 提出进程构造设计问题时写道 "In my FreeBSD patch, for example, I found there was already a status for a process in exec"，并说 "that was clean to reuse for one of these embryonic processes"，推测 Linux 应有类似概念。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1292909 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Kiryl Shutsemau 在 khugepaged mTHP 系列讨论里说 "it might be in these cases especially where the FreeBSD reservation system comes in handy"，即 FreeBSD 页面保留机制可为透明大页的最佳努力映射提供可借用思路。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1293325 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Mateusz Guzik 回复 Al Viro 时复述其结论 "Linux VFS is seriously different from Heidemann's-derived ones you'll find in BSD land these days"，并回说 So I keep finding，即 Linux VFS 与 BSD 系 vnode 分类差异是讨论落点。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1294318 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2020-04-24 linux-mm 讨论 RFC 补丁 mm Add PG_zero 时，Vlastimil Babka 指出背景清零并非首次被提出，他检索后发现 one BSD kernel 早在 2016 年就移除了该功能，并给出 DragonFly BSD 提交 afd2da4dc9056ea79 作为证据。原文引语: “found that one BSD kernel actually removed this functionality in 2016”。Matthew Wilcox 随即追问这是否意味着 BSD 使用了非临时存储以绕过 CPU 缓存。该邮件是 BSD 分支实现细节反向影响 Linux mm 清零策略讨论的直接证据。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1078447 -->`
- 【Linux社区】1998-11-19 linux-mm 早期邮件中，提问者询问 Linux 是否有计划实现 a la BSD 的整进程换页，答复者确认有且方案相当详细。该回复者随后评论这将在高负载下缩小 Linux 与 BSD 系内核的差距，并直言 BSD 社区常夸耀自身优势却忽略 SMP、ELF 与模块等 Linux 先行的领域。原文引语: “close the mouthes of some BSD people who talk loudly about”。这是 BSD 谱系对比的早期史料，显示 Linux VM 设计对 BSD 换页机制的自觉参照。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1078629 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2000-03-20 linux-mm 上讨论 MADV_SPACEAVAIL 与 MADV_FREE 时，Jamie Lokier 警告 MADV_DONTNEED 在不同操作系统上语义完全不同，Linux 的定义与 BSD 定义相悖，依赖 BSD 行为的程序可能在 Linux 上崩溃或产生无效结果。Chuck Lever 回应称更关心与 Solaris 的移植性，因服务器应用更多，但同意另设接口如 MADV_ZERO 提供 BSD 功能。原文引语: “your meaning of MADV_DONTNEED is different to BSD's”。这是 BSD 用户态内存接口语义直接影响 Linux madvise 设计决策的早期史料。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1079553 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2014-11-27 linux-mm 评审 Minchan Kim 的 mm 支持 madvise(MADV_FREE) 补丁时，Michal Hocko 询问 man 页是否跟进，并判断其语义应与 FreeBSD 相同或相近，直接给出 FreeBSD 的 madvise 手册链接 http://www.freebsd.org/cgi/man.cgi?query=madvise&sektion=2；补丁作者也说明 jemalloc 与 tcmalloc 早已在 FreeBSD 等系统支持该特性。原文引语: “I guess the semantic will be same/similar to FreeBSD”。这是 Linux 引入懒释放语义时以 FreeBSD 为参照系的明确证据。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1079558 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2000-05-24 linux-mm 讨论 2.3/4 版 VM 队列重构 RFC 时，Matthew Dillon（FreeBSD 与 DragonFly 主要开发者）以 FreeBSD 自身经验支持多队列方案，并详述 FreeBSD 4.x 的两遍 pageout 扫描算法与 PV entry 物理页老化机制，指出该老化设计移植到 Linux 是最难的部分。原文引语: “The multi-queue approach is the right way to go (for the same reason FBsd took that approach)”。这是 BSD VM 子系统的实现经验直接进入 Linux 内存管理设计讨论的典型谱系证据。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1080746 -->`
- 【Linux社区】2000-08-05 linux-mm 讨论新 VM 设计 RFC 时，Matthew Dillon 在邮件中提到 FreeBSD 社区似乎无人处理 4GB 以上的大内存配置，认为 IA32 上此类需求不大；Matthew Wilcox 随即反驳，称若有此需求应向 Oracle 或 SAP 求证。原文引语: “I don't think anyone in FreeBSDland has bothered with large-memory”。这显示 Linux 内存管理设计讨论中 BSD 阵营的实践边界被当作对照论据使用。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1080867 -->`
- 【Linux社区】FreeBSD 维护者 John Baldwin 以 FreeBSD 现场经验直接推动 Linux x86 ELF core note 设计。他自述在跑 FreeBSD 的 AMD Ryzen 9 5900X 桌面上发现 GDB 不识别 XSAVE core note, 因为 AMD 处理器 MPX 无空隙, PKRU 偏移与 Intel 不同。关键引语: "the XSAVE core dump notes were not recognized by GDB (FreeBSD dumps an XSAVE register set note"。该证据是 BSD 谱系与 Linux core dump ABI 交叉的直接史料, 判定 CAND。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1124496 -->`
- 【Linux社区】OpenBSD 创始人 Theo de Raadt 强力反对 Linux mseal 设计方向, 以 OpenBSD 工程经验定性其过早优化且有意不兼容。关键引语: "OpenBSD userland has zero places it can use mmap() MAP_IMMUTABLE"。他进一步自述花了整整一年工程化地最大化内存锁定场景, 并观察到 PROT_IMMUTABLE 草案演变为危险反模式。此为 BSD 谱系人物与 BSD 系统实证对 Linux 新系统调用的直接干预, 判定 CAND。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1124654 -->`
- 【Linux社区】mseal 系统调用 RFC 讨论中, 设计者直接以 OpenBSD 与 XNU 的不可变语义作为权威参照系来否定逐系统调用位掩码方案。关键引语: "The OpenBSD and XNU examples just say "This must be immutable""。该引语将 BSD 谱系的不可变内存模型确立为 Linux 新接口的评估基准, 构成 BSD 设计范式向 Linux ABI 的显式传导, 判定 CAND。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1124874 -->`
- 【Linux社区】mseal 补丁讨论中, 开发者以 OpenBSD 的 mimmutable 作为更优接口范本, 批评 Linux 方案过度复杂。关键引语: "OpenBSD's mimmutable() doesn't do any of this and it Just Works"。该引语把 BSD 谱系系统的具体接口作为 Linux 内存保护语义的设计标尺, 属 BSD 技术范式对 Linux ABI 的直接影响, 判定 CAND。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1125194 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Theo de Raadt就Linux mseal补丁提出OpenBSD视角反驳, 强调execve映射.data与.bss段时内核不会设置MAP_SEALABLE。引语: "like in OpenBSD) the kernel cannot and will not seal the .data section"。他还列举RELRO、IFUNCS与glibc将栈mprotect为可执行的现实约束, 称该设计为 "train wreck"。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1136914 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Andy Lutomirski提议彻底移除deny_write_access机制。引语: "It's broken, it's stupid, but we've done that ETXTBUSY for a _loong_ time"。讨论指出该机制源自BSD的deny_write_access, Linus承认已逐步削弱, BSD自身早已移除, 属BSD谱系代码在Linux中的残存。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1137415 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Liam R. Howlett回应Theo de Raadt对mmap与mseal交互的质疑, 引语: "OpenBSD won't be doing this"。Theo指出他曾在草案中做过PROT_IMMUTABLE, 但研究后发现"基本上没有任何情境"需要用户态在mmap时直接创建不可变映射, 反而形成"危险的反模式", 因此OpenBSD最终未采纳。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1137525 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Pedro Falcato回应Jann Horn关于fork时CoW造成子进程内存不一致的问题, 指出写保护路径也无法避免部分地址空间不一致, 必须挂起多线程才能修复。引语: "we would only be fixable if e.g we suspended every thread on a multi-threaded fork"。该讨论触及POSIX与Linux语义差异, 属跨内核API语义对照范畴。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1138252 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Jeff Xu引Theo de Raadt的OpenBSD视角说明mseal与sigaltstack的交互风险, 引语: "In OpenBSD, sigaltstack() forces MAP_STACK onto the specified (pre-allocated) region"。Theo称OpenBSD在syscall入口强制sp指向MAP_STACK区域, 能严重破坏ROP跳转方法, 而Linux尚无MAP_STACK强制机制, 建议Linux检查sigaltstack是否构成mseal的绕过路径。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1139187 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Waiman Long提议将MAP_STACK映射为VM_STACK以防止栈VMA与非栈匿名VMA合并。引语: "One of the flags of mmap(2) is MAP_STACK to request a memory segment suitable for a process or thread stack"。他指出glibc用MAP_STACK映射线程栈, 而Linux内核完全忽略该标志, 致使栈VMA可与相邻匿名VMA合并, 触发SELinux禁止可执行栈的检查失败并造成随机SEGV。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1141674 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Jeff Xu引Theo de Raadt解释BSD的immutable()语义, 引语: "It only prevents removal of the mapping, placement of a replacement mapping, or changing the existing permissions"。Theo明确拒绝与Linux的MM_SEAL系列标志做等价对照, 认为复杂度无必要; Jeff则确认若libc组合这些标志即等价于BSD的immutable()。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1141922 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Jeff Xu在mseal()系统调用首个RFC中询问BSD的immutable()实现范围, Theo de Raadt回复其只阻止映射移除与权限变更。引语: "It only prevents removal of the mapping, placement of a replacement mapping, or changing the existing permissions"。Jeff据此定义MM_IMMUTABLE宏组合, 称等价于BSD的immutable(); Theo则拒绝对照, 认为额外复杂度缺乏必要性。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1142189 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Jeff Xu 提交 mseal() RFC v3，把该调用放入 BSD 谱系语境：英文引语 "already exists in the XNU kernel with the VM_FLAGS_PERMANENT flag and on OpenBSD with the mimmutable syscall"。补丁说明称运行期加载器将自动封死 .text 与 .rodata 页面，Chrome 侧亦计划采纳用于 CFI 工作。这是 Linux 内存子系统显式移植 BSD 系不可变内存机制的原始提报，为 mseal 谱系链条的起点史料。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1145045 -->`
- 【Linux社区】OpenBSD 创始人 Theo de Raadt 介入 mseal RFC 评审，明确自述设计归属，英文引语 "I designed mimmutable(2) in OpenBSD, it took about a year to get all the components working correctly"，并称 Linux 版是 "a clone of mimmutable(2), but with an extremely over-complicated API based upon dubious arguments"。他给出的最终 API 只需传入地址与长度两个参数，以单一标志位实现不可变。这是 BSD 人物与 BSD 机制双要素的强证据。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1145309 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Theo de Raadt 就 mseal v7 的两项 mmap 标志给出 BSD 立场对照，英文引语 "OpenBSD won't be doing this. I had PROT_IMMUTABLE as a draft"，并援引调研结论指出用户态几乎不需要创建即封锁的映射，最常见的流程是先建立可读写映射、填充数据再收紧权限。他还援引 https://lwn.net/Articles/915662/ 阐述进程内锁页职责分层：内核占多数，其次共享库链接器，libc 极少，应用自身几乎只有一例。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1147238 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Theo de Raadt 正面回答 Jeff Xu 关于 BSD 实现的边界问题，Jeff 的英文原问 "Is BSD's definition of immutable equivalent" 指向四个 SEAL 标志，其答复界定 BSD 的 immutable 仅阻断映射移除、替换映射安置与既有权限变更，并解释 mlock 与 madvise 处理的是虚拟地址之下的物理内存，与利用地址空间构造提权链的攻击面无关。他还指出不可变对象在 OpenBSD 中被称为 entry。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1149012 -->`
- 【Linux社区】UDF 文件系统许可证清理补丁，评审意见引语 "you have removed the word, COPYRIGHT and in the other cases you have not"，另引语 "lean towards removing it, as I see it as a heading for the old license notice"。补丁主题为把 GPL 2.0 样板许可证声明替换为 SPDX 标识符，讨论核心是旧许可证文本中 COPYRIGHT 字样作为标题的去留，以及旧样板与 SPDX 标识的对应关系。该条目反映内核许可证标注治理实践。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1151393 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Linus Torvalds 回应 Theo de Raadt 对 mseal v10 的原子性异议，援引 Open Group 规范作为 POSIX 依据，引语 "When mmap() fails for reasons other than [EBADF], [EINVAL], or [ENOTSUP], some mappings ... may have been unmapped"，并给出跨谱系判断引语 "Maybe some atomicity rules have always been true for BSD, but they've never been true for Linux"。这是 BSD 与 Linux 语义差异被公开对照的最直接证据，同时记录了二人在评审中的直接冲突。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1151821 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Jeff Xu 就 mseal v8 反驳 Theo de Raadt 的安全模型假设，引语 "because you have cloned a memory subsystem primitive you don't fully understand"。de Raadt 一方以 malloc 后 mprotect 置为无访问权限再释放的示例说明 mseal 与 mimmutable 不给谨慎程序员增加任何新期望，其依据是这些工具只在承诺永不释放也永不改权的内存上使用。双方争论直指 Linux 机制与 BSD 原语的对齐程度。 `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1151941 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Jeff Xu's mseal() patchset v6 anchors the new memory sealing syscall on BSD prior art, citing OpenBSD's mimmutable syscall alongside XNU's VM_FLAGS_PERMANENT. Quote: A similar feature already exists in the XNU kernel and on OpenBSD with the mimmutable syscall. The Linux sealing proposal is documented as standing on the BSD side of the Unix lineage. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1214732 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Mateusz Guzik opens a Linux pidmap_lock IRQ disablement review by invoking classic BSD and Unix kernel interrupt raising primitives, singling out NetBSD as a BSD implementation with measurably slower amd64 interrupt level handling. Quote: I see NetBSD uses local cmpxchg8b on the interrupt level and interrupt mask. NetBSD is cited as a direct comparative reference for Linux kernel synchronization design. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1216819 -->`
- 【Linux社区】David Hildenbrand objects to Matthew Wilcox's small THP naming for sub-PMD-sized anonymous THP and invokes FreeBSD's vocabulary as an alternative precedent, noting FreeBSD already calls similar intermediate superpages medium-sized superpages. Quote: Maybe that's the reason why FreeBSD calls them medium-sized superpages. Linux developers reach out to FreeBSD terminology when standardizing naming for a new MM concept. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1217113 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Theo de Raadt, the OpenBSD founder and lead developer, directly participates in the Linux mseal() syscall review thread, engaging with Jeff Xu over the PROT_SEAL and MAP_SEALABLE mmap flag proposals. Quote: On Thu, Feb 1, 2024 at 8:10 PM Theo de Raadt deraadt@openbsd.org wrote. The OpenBSD lead directly reviews Linux kernel memory sealing proposals. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1217452 -->`
- 【Linux社区】David Hildenbrand reviews Weixi Zhu's vm_object abstraction for gmem and flags it as importing a BSD VM design pattern into Linux, then cites that the struct vm_object design originates from FreeBSD's unified VM abstraction. Quote: The idea of struct vm_object is originated from FreeBSD VM design. Linux reviewers explicitly name FreeBSD VM design when rejecting a new mm abstraction. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1217690 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Bagas Sanjaya's UDF SPDX conversion series skips fs/udf/ecma_167.h because its license text is a non-standard BSD variant combining BSD-2-Clause with a clause borrowed from BSD-3-Clause, making it inexpressible in SPDX. Quote: The latter file looks like 2-clause BSD Source-Code license, yet the second clause is from third clause of 3-Clause BSD. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1219958 -->`
- 【Linux社区】In the variable-order anonymous THP v6 cover-letter exchange, David Hildenbrand invokes FreeBSD vocabulary when naming small-sized THP, noting FreeBSD already calls similar intermediate superpages Medium-sized superpages. Quote: I think FreeBSD tends to call it Medium-sized superpages. Linux maintainers consistently reference FreeBSD naming when defining new large folio terminology. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1220899 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Linus Torvalds 2024-02-02 回复 Theo de Raadt (openbsd.org) 关于 mseal 系统调用原子性的 RFC 讨论, de Raadt 以 Unix 原语规范立场断言: Unix system calls must be atomic. Linus 反驳称该论断并不绝对, 并举 mlock 等反例, 讨论原子性作为 Linux 设计目标与 OpenBSD 一贯原则的差异. 属 BSD 代表人物直接进入 Linux 治理对话, 且明确以 BSD 语义基准为论点. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1222058 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Linus Torvalds 在 2024-05-14 关于 buffered IO 的 LSF/MM/BPF TOPIC 讨论中, 直接引用 mmap(2) 手册关于 MAP_STACK 的说明并明言该标志在其它系统上真实有效: MAP_STACK exists (and has an effect) on some other systems (e.g., some of the BSDs). 这是 Linux 侧开发讨论中对 BSD 家族系统行为差异的显式承认, 涉及跨 OS API 语义对比. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1222801 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Theo de Raadt 在 2023-12-14 回复 mseal 文档 RFC 时, 详细阐明 OpenBSD 对 .data 与 bss 段的默认不可变策略: The OpenBSD kernel makes those regions immutable BY DEFAULT, 并展示其新增的 ELF program header OPENBSD_MUTABLE 与 section .openbsd.mutable, 用于在 libc malloc 与 chrome v8 v8_flags 等场景下显式标注可写对象, 是 OpenBSD 独有 ELF 机制向 Linux 社区输出的完整实例. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1226110 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Andrew Morton 在 2023-04-20 关于 MAP_STACK 到 VM_STACK 映射的讨论中直接引用 mmap(2) 手册并明言该标志在其它系统真实存在且生效: MAP_STACK exists (and has an effect) on some of the BSDs. Linux 当前将其视为 no-op 仅用于将来兼容与移植性, 是 BSD 家族与 Linux 语义差异的直接书面证据. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1226857 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Bagas Sanjaya 2023-05-30 提交的 UDF 文件系统 SPDX 转换系列被 Jan Kara 入队. 提案中明确指出 fs/udf/ecma_167.h 使用了一种无法用 SPDX 标识符表达的 BSD 变体: The latter file apparently looks like 2-clause BSD Source-Code license, yet the second clause is from third clause of 3-Clause BSD. 该文件因此被排除在转换之外, 是 BSD 许可证文本在 Linux 内核树中的遗留与分类实例. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1227080 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Florian Weimer 2023-01-09 在 glibc arc4random 讨论中, 说明 chrony 若改为调用 glibc 版 arc4random (放弃自带 userspace buffering) 时性能显著下降: if chrony is recompiled against the glibc version of arc4random (instead of its OpenBSD compat version, which uses userspace buffering), the result is a 25% drop in NTP packet response rate. 是 BSD 派生工具在 Linux 用户态的兼容实现证据. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1227180 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Jeff Xu 2023-10-17 提交 mseal() 系统调用 RFC 时, 在提案说明中显式对比 BSD 家族已有实现: A similar feature already exists in the XNU kernel with the VM_FLAGS_PERMANENT flag and on OpenBSD with the mimmutable syscall. 该 syscall 最终以 mseal 合入, 是 OpenBSD mimmutable 谱系进入 Linux 内核的直接书面证据. `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1227542 -->`
- 【Linux社区】提问者在邮件列表中询问是否存在将文件映射入其他进程地址空间的原语,并明确对比 XNU 与 Windows 以同一方式直接实现、Linux 无等价机制,是 BSD 谱系 mach_vm_map 与 vm_map 技术对照的一手史料。英文引语:"Both XNU and Windows do this directly" `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1295062 -->`
- 【Linux社区】David Hildenbrand 在讨论软件页表方案时,以 SunOS 与 UVM 的 amap 中间结构为历史参照,指出这些经典 UNIX 系统中匿名内存状态与页表完全冗余,是 BSD 4.4 VM 谱系与 Linux 页表格式差异的一手比较。英文引语:"SunOS and UVM call it amap" `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1295214 -->`
- 【Linux社区】讨论 kmalloc_obj 类型化 slab 缓存时引用 iOS 与 XNU 的安全实践,说明 Apple 为每种 kmalloc 类型建立独立 slab 缓存以强化内存安全,构成 BSD 派生内核 XNU 影响 Linux mm 设计的一手佐证。英文引语:"one slab cache for each type of kmalloc" `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1297211 -->`
- 【Linux社区】关于 selftests/exec 中 SPDX 许可证标识符的判读争议:代码文本被确认为 OpenBSD 许可而非 ISC,作者比对 Linux 树内 LICENSES 目录后询问正确的 SPDX 标识符,是 BSD 许可在 Linux 内核中的直接落地案例。英文引语:"The license is OpenBSD, not ISC." `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1297540 -->`
- 【Linux社区】在 mTHP khugepaged 讨论中以 FreeBSD 的预留机制为正面参照,主张最佳努力分配 THP、真正变热后再映射,并允许回收时拆散,构成 FreeBSD 与 Linux THP 策略的直接对照。英文引语:"FreeBSD reservation system comes in handy" `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1299004 -->`
- 【Linux社区】generic_fadvise 校验补丁以 FreeBSD 行为为一致性论据,主张对负 offset 返回 EINVAL 与既有实践一致,是 FreeBSD POSIX 语义影响 Linux mm API 决策的直接一手史料。英文引语:"FreeBSD rejects negative offsets as well" `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1300367 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Christoph Lameter 以 iOS 与 XNU 的实践为证,说明 Apple 出于安全考虑为每种 kmalloc 类型建立独立 slab 缓存,为 Linux kmalloc_obj 类型化分配提案提供 BSD 派生内核的对照依据。英文引语:"one slab cache for each type of kmalloc" `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1302103 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Al Viro 在解析 iput 与 writeback 死锁问题时给出 Linux VFS 对象模型说明,明确指出 Linux VFS 与当代 BSD 世界在对象分类学上严重不同,是 Linux 与 BSD VFS 谱系差异的权威一手陈述。英文引语:"you'll find in BSD land these days" `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1304069 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Mateusz Guzik 以 FreeBSD 大规模包构建经验指出,尽管 FreeBSD 常被质疑扩展性不足,但 Linux 同样存在 rmap 共享带来的执行期锁争用问题,是 FreeBSD 工程实践直接支撑 Linux mm 提案的一手史料。英文引语:"I have extensive experience with this crap on FreeBSD" `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1308160 -->`
- 【Linux社区】关于 iput 与 writeback 死锁讨论的延续,Al Viro 强调 Linux dentry 与 inode 语义不同于 vnode 传统,并明确称 Linux VFS 与当代 BSD 世界在对象分类学上截然不同,为 BSD VFS 谱系对照提供权威文本。英文引语:"Different taxonomy of objects" `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1312071 -->`
- 【Linux社区】作者回复 fadvise 负偏移校验补丁的复审请求,同意补充 Linux man page、POSIX 与 FreeBSD 行为对比后重发,证明 FreeBSD 语义被用作 Linux mm API 变更的合规参照。英文引语:"linux manpages, POSIX, freebsd etc" `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1312432 -->`
- 【Linux社区】Andrew Morton 在合并 fadvise 负偏移校验补丁时引用 FreeBSD 拒绝负偏移的实践作为行为一致性依据,并要求作者补全 man page、POSIX 与 FreeBSD 对照说明,体现 BSD 对 Linux 语义决策的影响。英文引语:"FreeBSD rejects negative offsets I guess we are good" `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1312652 -->`
- 【Linux社区】David Laight 在讨论 setuid 脚本安全时追溯 shell 语法分析历史,区分原始 1970 年代 sh、SVR 与 SunOS 版本及现代 shell,涉及 BSD 与 SysV 分支谱系的技术史细节。英文引语:"not even the SVSV/Sunos version" `<!-- ROW_MINE: linux-mm.messages.1312875 -->`
